    );
    assert_eq!(
        read(
            &mut e,
            "statusCount",
            "carol",
            json!({"status":"closed"}),
            1000
        ),
        json!({"count":null})
    );
    assert!(
    assert!(
        read(
            &mut e,
            "privateTicket",
            "bob",
            json!({"ticket":r["id"]}),
            1000
        )
        .is_null()
    );
}
#[test]
fn live_parent_transfer_moves_existing_child_authority() {
    let mut e = engine("table movableFolders:");
    let f = write(&mut e, "newMovableFolder", "alice", json!({}), 1000);
    let r = write(
        &mut e,
        "liveFile",
        "alice",
        json!({"folder":f["id"],"body":"private"}),
        1000,
    );
    let args = json!({"file":r["id"]});
    assert!(!read(&mut e, "liveFileById", "alice", args.clone(), 1000).is_null());
    assert!(read(&mut e, "liveFileById", "bob", args.clone(), 1000).is_null());
    write(
        &mut e,
        "transferFolder",
        "alice",
        json!({"folder":f["id"],"who":"bob"}),
        1000,
    );
    assert!(read(&mut e, "liveFileById", "alice", args.clone(), 1000).is_null());
    assert!(!read(&mut e, "liveFileById", "bob", args, 1000).is_null());
}
#[test]
fn publication_needs_ten_distinct_irrevocable_approvals() {
    let mut e = engine("table petitionPublications:");
    let p = write(
        &mut e,
        "petition",
        "alice",
        json!({"body":"private until threshold"}),
        1000,
    );
    let args = json!({"petition":p["id"]});
    fail(
        &mut e,
        "publishPetition",
        "alice",
        args.clone(),
        1000,
        "TOO_FEW",
    );
    for n in 0..9 {
        write(&mut e, "approve", &format!("voter{n}"), args.clone(), 1000);
    }
    fail(
        &mut e,
        "publishPetition",
        "alice",
        args.clone(),
        1000,
        "TOO_FEW",
    );
    write(&mut e, "approve", "voter9", args.clone(), 1000);
    write(&mut e, "publishPetition", "alice", args.clone(), 1000);
    assert_eq!(
        read(&mut e, "petitionPublication", "outsider", args, 1000)["body"],
        "private until threshold"
    );
}
#[test]
fn closed_ballots_expose_only_the_maintained_result() {
    let mut e = engine("table ballots:");
    let p = write(&mut e, "poll", "alice", json!({}), 1000);
    let args = json!({"poll":p["id"],"choice":"yes"});
    write(&mut e, "vote", "bob", args.clone(), 1000);
    assert_eq!(
        read(&mut e, "ballotResult", "carol", args.clone(), 1000),
        json!({"count":null})
    );
    assert!(read(&mut e, "ownBallot", "carol", json!({"poll":p["id"]}), 1000).is_null());
    write(&mut e, "closePoll", "alice", json!({"poll":p["id"]}), 1000);
    assert_eq!(
        read(&mut e, "ballotResult", "carol", args.clone(), 1000),
        json!({"count":1})
    );
    assert_eq!(
        read(
            &mut e,
            "ballotResult",
            "carol",
            json!({"poll":p["id"],"choice":"no"}),
            1000
        ),
        json!({"count":0})
    );
    fail(&mut e, "vote", "carol", args, 1000, "E_RULE");
}
#[test]
fn presence_copies_scope_and_revokes_with_enrolment() {
    let mut e = engine("table presenceRows:");
    seed(
        &mut e,
        "spaces",
        json!({"id":"s","title":"private participation"}),
    );
    for who in ["alice", "bob"] {
        seed(
            &mut e,
            "enrolments",
            json!({"id":who,"space":"s","principal":who}),
        );
    }
    write(&mut e, "pulse", "alice", json!({"space":"s"}), 1000);
    let args = json!({"space":"s","who":"alice"});
    assert!(!read(&mut e, "presence", "bob", args.clone(), 1000).is_null());
    assert!(read(&mut e, "presence", "carol", args.clone(), 1000).is_null());
    write(&mut e, "leaveSpace", "bob", json!({"space":"s"}), 1000);
    assert!(read(&mut e, "presence", "bob", args.clone(), 1000).is_null());
    fail(
        &mut e,
        "pulse",
        "bob",
        json!({"space":"s"}),
        1000,
        "NOT_FOUND",
    );
    assert!(read(&mut e, "presence", "alice", args, 5000).is_null());
}
#[test]
fn system_job_comparison_does_not_publish_scores() {
    let mut e = engine("table secretScores:");
    let a = write(&mut e, "secretScore", "alice", json!({"score":9}), 1000);
    let b = write(&mut e, "secretScore", "bob", json!({"score":2}), 1000);
    assert!(
        read(
            &mut e,
            "secretScoreById",
            "carol",
            json!({"score":a["id"]}),
            1000
        )
        .is_null()
    );
    let args = [
        ("left".into(), Value::Id(a["id"].as_str().unwrap().into())),
        ("right".into(), Value::Id(b["id"].as_str().unwrap().into())),
    ]
    .into();
    assert!(matches!(
        e.run_job("compare", args, 1000).unwrap().state,
        JobState::Done
    ));
    let rows = e.store().rows("comparisons").unwrap();
    assert_eq!(rows.len(), 1);
    let key = wire::to_json(rows[0].field("id").unwrap());
    let r = read(
        &mut e,
        "comparison",
        "carol",
        json!({"comparison":key}),
        1000,
    );
    assert_eq!(r["leftHigher"], true);
    assert!(r.get("score").is_none());
}
#[test]
fn invitation_enrols_without_reading_the_hidden_parent() {
    let mut e = engine("table hiddenClubs:");
    let c = write(&mut e, "club", "alice", json!({"title":"private"}), 1000);
    let args = json!({"club":c["id"]});
    assert!(read(&mut e, "hiddenClub", "bob", args.clone(), 1000).is_null());
    fail(&mut e, "acceptClub", "bob", args.clone(), 1000, "NOT_FOUND");
    write(
        &mut e,
        "inviteClub",
        "alice",
        json!({"club":c["id"],"who":"bob"}),
        1000,
    );
    assert!(read(&mut e, "hiddenClub", "bob", args.clone(), 1000).is_null());
    write(&mut e, "acceptClub", "bob", args.clone(), 1000);
    assert_eq!(
        read(&mut e, "hiddenClub", "bob", args.clone(), 1000)["title"],
        "private"
    );
    assert!(read(&mut e, "hiddenClub", "carol", args, 1000).is_null());
}
#[test]
fn media_link_revocation_and_expiry_have_separate_custody() {
    let mut e = engine("table mediaDocuments:");
    let d = write(
        &mut e,
        "mediaDocument",
        "alice",
        json!({"title":"private image"}),
        1000,
    );
    let asset = e.store_asset("alice", PNG, 1000).unwrap();
    let asset = e.store_asset("alice", PNG, 1000).unwrap();
    write(
        &mut e,
        "placeMedia",
        "alice",
        json!({"document":d["id"],"image":wire::to_json(&asset.column_value())}),
        1000,
    );
    assert!(e.asset_access("bob", &asset.id, 1000).is_err());
    let secret = "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef";
    write(
        &mut e,
        "mediaLink",
        "service:ops",
        json!({"document":d["id"],"secret":secret,"expiresAt":2000}),
        1000,
    );
    write(&mut e, "claimMedia", "bob", json!({"secret":secret}), 1000);
    let args = json!({"document":d["id"]});
    assert!(!read(&mut e, "mediaDocumentById", "bob", args.clone(), 1000).is_null());
    assert!(e.asset_access("bob", &asset.id, 1000).is_ok());
    write(
        &mut e,
        "revokeMediaLink",
        "service:ops",
        json!({"secret":secret}),
        1000,
    );
    fail(
        &mut e,
        "claimMedia",
        "carol",
        json!({"secret":secret}),
        1000,
        "BAD_LINK",
    );
    assert!(!read(&mut e, "mediaDocumentById", "bob", args.clone(), 1000).is_null());
    write(
        &mut e,
        "revokeMediaReader",
        "service:ops",
        json!({"document":d["id"],"who":"bob"}),
        1000,
    );
    assert!(read(&mut e, "mediaDocumentById", "bob", args.clone(), 1000).is_null());
    assert!(e.asset_access("bob", &asset.id, 1000).is_err());
    let secret2 = "fedcba9876543210fedcba9876543210";
    write(
        &mut e,
        "mediaLink",
        "service:ops",
        json!({"document":d["id"],"secret":secret2,"expiresAt":2000}),
        1000,
    );
    write(
        &mut e,
        "claimMedia",
        "carol",
        json!({"secret":secret2}),
        1000,
    );
    assert!(!read(&mut e, "mediaDocumentById", "carol", args.clone(), 1999).is_null());
    assert!(read(&mut e, "mediaDocumentById", "carol", args.clone(), 2000).is_null());
    assert!(e.asset_access("carol", &asset.id, 2000).is_err());
    assert!(!read(&mut e, "mediaDocumentById", "alice", args, 2000).is_null());
}

#[test]
fn public_metadata_self_join_does_not_publish_private_payload() {
    let mut e = engine("table clubCatalogue:");
    for open in [false, true] {
        let c = write(
            &mut e,
            "catalogueClub",
            "alice",
            json!({"title":"visible","open":open,"body":"private"}),
            1000,
        );
        let args = json!({"club":c["id"]});
        assert_eq!(
            read(&mut e, "catalogueClubById", "bob", args.clone(), 1000)["title"],
            "visible"
        );
        assert!(read(&mut e, "clubSheet", "bob", args.clone(), 1000).is_null());
        if open {
            write(&mut e, "selfJoinClub", "bob", args.clone(), 1000);
            assert_eq!(
                read(&mut e, "clubSheet", "bob", args, 1000)["body"],
                "private"
            );
        } else {
            fail(&mut e, "selfJoinClub", "bob", args, 1000, "E_RULE");
        }
    }
}
#[test]
fn hidden_only_page_is_complete_empty_and_never_a_denial() {
    let source = "table readers:\n  scope: text <=20\n  principal: principal\n  unique scope, principal\n  read <- .principal = viewer\n  online only\ntable secrets:\n  scope: text <=20\n  body: text <=80\n  read <- exists readers[.scope, viewer]\n  online only\nquery page(c: cursor ?):\n  return secrets where .scope = 'private' first 20 by .id after c\n";
    let b = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
    assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", b.diagnostics);
    let mut e = Engine::memory(b.schema, b.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
    seed(
        &mut e,
        "secrets",
        json!({"id":"s","scope":"private","body":"hidden"}),
    );
    let c = ctx(&e, "page", "bob", json!({"c":null}), 1000);
    let page = e.query("page", c).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(wire::to_json(&page.data), json!([]));
    assert!(page.complete);
    assert!(page.next.is_none());
    assert!(page.cut.is_none());
}

//! Binding: documents in, a core schema and typed programs out, with every
//! problem as a located diagnostic.

pub mod catalog;
mod columns;
mod ephemeral;
mod defaults;
mod first_paste;
mod maintains;
mod own_keys;
mod refusals;
pub(crate) mod rule_operator;
mod pin_rewrite;
mod complete_form;
mod auth_lints;
mod owner_equality;
mod write_affordances;
mod rule_composition;

use crate::ast::Document;
use crate::diag::Diagnostic;
use catalog::Catalog;
pub use program::BoundProgram;
pub use aggregate_copies::truth as rule_truth;
use snapback4_core::schema::Schema;

pub struct Bound {
    /// Receipt member operation names, needed only by TypeScript emission.
    pub receipt_members: std::collections::BTreeMap<String, String>,
    /// Compiler-side source map; never serialized into the client backend.
    pub refusal_sites: std::collections::BTreeMap<(String, String), String>,
    pub view_types: std::collections::BTreeMap<String, crate::types::Ty>,
    /// Authoring-only opt-in; never stored in the core schema, IR or wire.
    pub server: bool,
    pub schema: Schema,
    pub programs: Vec<BoundProgram>,
    pub personas: Vec<String>,
    pub diagnostics: Vec<Diagnostic>,
    /// Facts the compiler can state that are not errors: a page that is
    /// never `complete` because its read rule is not decided by its prefix.
    pub notes: Vec<Diagnostic>,
}

pub fn bind(documents: Vec<Document>) -> Bound {
    let mut bound = bind_unverified(documents.clone());
    crate::repair::verify_bound(&documents, &mut bound.diagnostics);
    bound
}

// Repair checks run the same planner and admission, without recursively
// verifying the diagnostics from a rejected candidate.
pub(crate) fn bind_unverified(mut documents: Vec<Document>) -> Bound {
    let mut diagnostics = Vec::new();
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#8 — catalog provenance comes from source, before any generated declarations.
    let authored_indexes = documents.iter().flat_map(|doc|
        doc.tables.iter().map(|t| (&t.name, &t.indexes))
            .chain(doc.maintains.iter().map(|m| (&m.name, &m.indexes))))
        .map(|(name, indexes)| (name.clone(), indexes.iter().map(|i| i.name.clone()).collect())).collect();
    receipts::expand(&mut documents, &mut diagnostics);
    ephemeral::declarations(&documents, &mut diagnostics);
    helper_names::normalize(&mut documents, &mut diagnostics);
    let server = documents.iter().any(|doc| doc.server);
    server::admit(&mut documents, &mut diagnostics);
    let feeds = feed_view::expand(&mut documents, &mut diagnostics);
    // Following expansion adds its durable timeline inputs. Recheck before lowering.
    if !feeds.is_empty() { ephemeral::declarations(&documents, &mut diagnostics); }
    let documents = timeline::expand(documents, &mut diagnostics);
    let personas: Vec<String> = documents.iter().flat_map(|d| d.personas.iter().cloned()).collect();
    let mut catalog = Catalog::build(documents, &mut diagnostics);
    catalog.feed_views = feeds;
    page_shapes::hoist(&mut catalog);
    mixed_keys::normalize(&mut catalog);
    intent::table_order(&mut catalog);
    intent_aggregates::remember_domains(&mut catalog);
    intent_search::capture(&mut catalog);
    intent_variants::expand(&mut catalog);
    intent_views::expand(&mut catalog, &mut diagnostics);
    // After argument-presence variants: `x = null or …` stays theirs (cursor validation across variants).
    where_guards::lower(&mut catalog);
    let stable_fields = catalog.tables.iter().map(|(name, table)| {
        let mut fields: std::collections::BTreeSet<String> = table.decl.columns.iter()
            .filter(|column| column.written_by.is_none() && column.maintained.is_none()).map(|column| column.name.clone()).collect();
        fields.insert("id".into()); (name.clone(), fields)
    }).collect();
    for doc in &mut catalog.documents { for program in &mut doc.programs { stable_page_keys::lower_program(program, &stable_fields); } }
    intent::plan(&mut catalog, &mut diagnostics);
    reference_actions::indexes(&mut catalog);
    let mut schema = schema::bind_schema(&catalog, &mut diagnostics);
    schema.authored_indexes = snapback4_core::schema::inspection::AuthoredIndexes(Some(authored_indexes));
    timeline::admit(&catalog, &mut schema, &mut diagnostics);
    delivery::bind(&catalog, &mut schema, &mut diagnostics);
    receipts::bind(&catalog, &mut schema, &mut diagnostics);
    schema.prepare_receipt_dependencies();
    reference_actions::bind(&catalog, &mut schema, &mut diagnostics);
    // @ref LLP 3000.015#1 — timeline mappings exist only after lowering both spellings.
    for issue in snapback4_core::schema::field_authority::validate(&schema) {
        if !diagnostics.iter().any(|d| d.code == "E_FIELD_AUTHORITY" && d.message == issue.message) {
            diagnostics.push(crate::diag::Diagnostic::new(&catalog.site_for(&issue.path), "E_FIELD_AUTHORITY", issue.message,
                "declare readable by audience before using a private field in a timeline mapping or predicate").guide("rules"));
        }
    }
    // Timeline ownership is resolved after the ordinary schema checks.
    for issue in snapback4_core::schema::columns::timeline_issues(&schema) {
                    diagnostics.push(crate::diag::unknown(&decl.site, format!("API name {root} is both an operation and a module; first declared at {first_site}"), "rename the bare operation or the module file"));
                    continue;
                }
                api_roots.entry(root.clone()).or_insert((nested, decl.site.clone()));
            }
            if let Some(bound) = program::Binder::bind(&catalog, &schema, &module, decl, &mut diagnostics) {
                programs.push(bound);
            }
        }
    }
    actions::bind(&catalog, &mut schema, &mut programs, &mut diagnostics);
    routes::bind(&catalog, &mut schema, &mut diagnostics);
    if diagnostics.is_empty() { intent_search::provision(&mut catalog, &mut schema, &programs); }
    // Planner-added indexes participate in the same final source prices.
    schema.export_write_reservations();
    // The residency facts come before admission: a never-predicting mutation may pass over a uniformly authorized page (LLP 3000.007 §5.9), and admission reads that class here.
    let mut receipt_notes = Vec::new();
    schema.operations = programs.iter().map(|bound| {
        let mut fact = residency::execution_with_presence(&schema, &bound.program, bound.presence.as_ref());
        residency::locate_sites(&catalog, bound, &mut fact);
        if bound.program.kind == snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Mutation {
            let mut timed = false;
            for site in &mut fact.sites {
                if schema.table(&site.table).is_some_and(|table| table.not_before.is_some()) {
                    timed = true;
                    site.class = snapback4_core::schema::SiteClass::Server;
                    site.reason = format!("{} has `not before`; only the server resolves and changes scheduled rows", site.table);
                    site.rewrite = "queue this mutation while offline; a device clock never authorizes scheduled payloads".into();
                }
            }
            if timed { fact.predicts = Some("never".into()); }
        }
        if let Some((site, location)) = residency::receipts::located(&schema, bound, &catalog) {
            fact.predicts = Some("never".into());
            fact.sites.retain(|s| s.kind != "receipt dependency");
            let generated = schema.receipts.iter().any(|f| [f.delivered.as_str(), f.seen.as_str(), f.compact.as_str()].contains(&bound.program.name.as_str()));
            if !generated {
                receipt_notes.push(Diagnostic::new(&location, "N_RECEIPT_PREDICTION", format!("mutation {} predicts: never; queued without a row: {}", bound.program.name, site.reason), &site.rewrite).guide("receipts"));
            }
            fact.sites.insert(0, site);
        }
        (bound.program.name.clone(), fact)
    }).collect();
    for doc in &catalog.documents {
        let module = std::path::Path::new(&doc.file).file_stem().unwrap().to_string_lossy();
        for decl in &doc.programs {
            if decl.held && let Some(fact) = schema.operations.get_mut(&operation_name(&module, &decl.name)) {
                fact.held = true;
                fact.components.clear();
                fact.runs = Some("device".into());
            }
            if decl.server && let Some(fact) = schema.operations.get_mut(&operation_name(&module, &decl.name)) {
                fact.predicts = Some("never".into());
                fact.sites.push(snapback4_core::schema::ResidencySite { site: decl.site.to_string(), table: "server".into(), index: "".into(), kind: "dispatch".into(), class: snapback4_core::schema::SiteClass::Server, reason: "author marked this mutation : server".into(), rewrite: "remove : server to allow prediction when every site is held".into() });
            }
        }
    }
    ephemeral::programs(&catalog, &mut schema, &programs, &mut diagnostics);
    delivery::durability(&catalog, &schema, &mut diagnostics);
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20 — predicts asserts the existing eligibility decision; it never changes residency.
    for doc in &catalog.documents {
        let module = std::path::Path::new(&doc.file).file_stem().unwrap().to_string_lossy();
        for decl in doc.programs.iter().filter(|decl| decl.predicts) {
            let name = operation_name(&module, &decl.name);
            if let Some(fact) = schema.operations.get(&name) && fact.predicts.as_deref() == Some("never")
                && let Some(site) = fact.sites.iter().find(|site| site.class == snapback4_core::schema::SiteClass::Server) {
                diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(&decl.site, "E_NOT_PREDICTABLE", format!("mutation {name} asserts predicts, but {}", residency::prediction_reason(&schema, site)), &site.rewrite).guide("prediction"));
            }
        }
    }
    if diagnostics.is_empty() {
        let scopes = programs.iter().map(|bound| (&bound.program, &bound.maintenance_scopes)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        for (i, message) in snapback4_core::engine::admission::check_state_predicate_scopes(&schema, &scopes) {
            diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(&programs[i].site, "E_VIEW_KEY", message,
                "project value or member fields before computing; use a separate variable for the row").guide("maintains"));
        }
    }
    if diagnostics.is_empty() {
        programs.retain(|bound| match snapback4_core::engine::admission::check_detail_with_statements(&schema, &bound.program, &bound.statement_ends) {
            Ok(()) => true,
            Err(error) => {
                if let Some(diagnostic) = bound.expiry_gap_diagnostic(&schema, &error).or_else(|| bound.constraint_diagnostic(&schema, &error)) {
                    diagnostics.push(diagnostic);
                    return false;
                }
                let source = bound.sources.get(&error.site).or_else(|| error.page_site.as_ref().and_then(|site| bound.sources.get(site)));
                let site = source.map_or(&bound.site, |(site, _)| site);
                let message = if error.site.starts_with("for/") && let Some((_, name)) = source { format!("loop over {name}: {}", error.message) } else { error.message };
                let mut rewrite = error.rewrite;
                if rewrite.starts_with("iterate the original group:") && let Some(page) = error.page_site.as_ref().and_then(|site| bound.sources.get(site))
                    && let Some(scan) = snapback4_core::ir::scans(&bound.program).into_iter().find(|scan| Some(&scan.site) == error.page_site.as_ref()) {
                    let group = whole_group_rewrite(&catalog, &page.1, scan);
                    rewrite = format!("iterate the original group: `for row in {group}:`; put the condition and work inside that loop");
                }
                diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(site, error.code, message, rewrite).guide("programs"));
                false
            }
        });
    }
    for doc in &catalog.documents {
        for decl in &doc.schedules {
            let target = decl.target.strip_prefix("schema.").unwrap_or(&decl.target);
            if schema.operations.get(target).is_some_and(|f| f.ephemeral) {
                diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(&decl.site, "E_QUARRY_EPHEMERAL", "schedule cannot call an ephemeral mutation", "call the ephemeral mutation from the client").guide("ephemeral"));
                continue;
            }
            match bind_schedule(decl, &programs) {
                Ok(schedule) => schema.schedules.push(schedule),
                Err(mut diagnostic) => {
                    // A declared job that failed to bind already has its own error; this one only follows from it.
                    if !programs.iter().any(|p| p.program.name == decl.target)
                        && let Some(failed) = catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|doc| doc.programs.iter()).find(|p| p.name == target || decl.target.ends_with(&format!(".{}", p.name))) {
                        diagnostic.cascade_of = Some(failed.site.clone());
                        // @ref LLP 3000.036#15.7 — lane 039: the job's own problem is the one to fix;
                        // this follows from it, so it reads as a cascade and hides beside its cause.
                        let end = catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|doc| doc.programs.iter())
                            .filter(|p| p.site.file == failed.site.file && p.site.line > failed.site.line).map(|p| p.site.line).min().unwrap_or(u32::MAX);
                        if let Some(cause) = diagnostics.iter().find(|d| d.site.file == failed.site.file && (failed.site.line..end).contains(&d.site.line) && d.cascade_of.is_none()) {
                            diagnostic.message = format!("schedule {} names {}, which did not compile (its problem is at {})", decl.name, decl.target, cause.site);
                            diagnostic.rewrite = "fix this Quarry line and rerun snapback4 check".into();
                            diagnostic.cascade_of = Some(cause.site.clone());
                        }
                    }
                    diagnostics.push(diagnostic)
                }
            }
        }
    }
    schema.operations.retain(|name, _| programs.iter().any(|bound| &bound.program.name == name));
    schema.action_specs.retain(|name, _| programs.iter().any(|bound| &bound.program.name == name && bound.program.kind == snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Action));
    let mut notes: Vec<_> = programs.iter().flat_map(|p| p.notes.clone()).chain(catalog.planner_notes.clone()).collect();
    notes.extend(receipt_notes);
    notes.extend(ephemeral::notes(&schema, &programs));
    notes.extend(completeness_notes(&catalog, &schema, &programs));
    notes.extend(denied_reads(&catalog, &schema, &programs));
    notes.extend(maintain_authorization::notes(&catalog, &schema));
    // @ref LLP 3000.036#16.4 — wave 6 #22: authorization shapes that compile but grant or withhold more than meant.
    notes.extend(auth_lints::notes(&catalog, &schema));
    notes.extend(write_affordances::notes(&catalog, &schema));
    notes.extend(rule_composition::notes(&catalog));
    notes.extend(optional_patches::notes(&catalog));
    notes.extend(residency::maintain_notes(&catalog, &schema));
    notes.extend(columns::notes(&catalog, &schema));
    notes.extend(residency::guard_notes(&schema, &programs));
    notes.extend(residency::sync_order_notes(&schema, &programs));
    notes.extend(residency::empty_partition_notes(&schema, &programs));
    notes.extend(held::notes(&schema, &programs));
    notes.extend(timeline::notes(&catalog, &schema));
    if diagnostics.is_empty() { notes.extend(aggregate_copies::notes(&catalog, &schema)); }
    index_diagnostics::authored(&catalog, &mut diagnostics);
    index_diagnostics::authored(&catalog, &mut notes);

 ```
 
 Public visitors still need transport authentication: declare `use identity`, call `auth.guest()` from `snapback4/react`'s `useAuth()` (or `client.guest()`), then query. Public describes row authority, not an unauthenticated HTTP door. A guest can see shared rows, not team rows; password accounts enroll in the team roster. Use `use identity password` when the app should have no visitors.
+
+## Day-scoped and time-window visibility
+
+Store the assigned day as a key in a roster; select it in the API instead of
+putting a clock in a rule. A publication's lower and upper lifetime bounds are
+server enforced. Here staff assign readers, and only the assigned day reader
+can read the publication while it is live. Changing the roster revokes access
+on the next read; the day never changes on an existing publication.
+
+```quarry
+table dayReaders:
+  principal: principal
+  dayNumber: int
+  unique principal, dayNumber
+  immutable principal, dayNumber
+  read <- .principal = viewer or viewer = 'service:ops'
+  insert <- viewer = 'service:ops'
+  delete <- viewer = 'service:ops'
+  online only
+table dayPublications:
+  dayNumber: int
+  opensAt: time
+  endsAt: time
+  body: text 1..200
+  by byOpening: opensAt
+  by byExpiry: endsAt
+  immutable dayNumber, opensAt, endsAt, body
+  not before .opensAt
+  expire at .endsAt
+  read <- exists dayReaders[viewer, .dayNumber]
+  insert <- viewer = 'service:ops'
+  online only
+mutation assignDay(who: principal, dayNumber: int):
+  require viewer = 'service:ops' else NOT_OPERATOR
+  return insert dayReaders { principal: who, dayNumber }
+mutation publishDay(dayNumber: int, opensAt: time, endsAt: time, body: text 1..200):
+  require viewer = 'service:ops' else NOT_OPERATOR
+  require opensAt < endsAt else BAD_WINDOW
+  return insert dayPublications { dayNumber, opensAt, endsAt, body }
+query dayPublication(publication: dayPublications):
+  return dayPublications[publication]
+```
+
+For a slot-relative audience (the previous speaker), store an immutable slot
+identity and a validated successor principal, then authorize that principal
+explicitly. A lookup of a hidden prior slot cannot repair its successor. Give
+that maintenance operation readable authority, or update by key under an
+explicit transition rule; do not silently skip a nullable hidden lookup.
+
+## Column-level privacy: public identity, private sheet
+
+A returned shape does not protect a stored field. Split the row so that every
+field in the public identity is public and the private sheet has its own read
+rule. Column-level read rules remain an open language decision; this recipe applies ordinary row rules.
+
+```quarry
+table identities:
+  principal: principal
+  name: text 1..80
+  unique principal
+  immutable principal
+  public 'display name and account principal are public'
+  insert <- .principal = viewer
+  online only
+table privateSheets:
+  identity: identities
+  owner: principal

Only expose claimMedia as the reader writer. A generic insert of a grant would
bypass possession and expiry checks. Repeated claims use the declared unique
constraint; they do not extend a grant's expiry.

## N_LOOKUP_NEVER_VISIBLE

N_LOOKUP_NEVER_VISIBLE means a nullable point lookup is always null for callers
satisfying its preceding require/branch conditions: those conditions contradict
the table read rule, so a non-null branch cannot run. Authorize the read or
write by key under the table rule; Enrolment into hidden things shows the latter.
Unknown relationships and role overlap do not trigger this proof.

### Self-join through a public catalogue

The public catalogue is the root metadata; the private payload has a separate
row rule. Open entries permit self-join without an invitation. Closed entries
refuse the join even though their metadata is readable. Opening an entry is
an explicit owner decision, not a consequence of knowing its id.

```quarry
table clubCatalogue:
  owner: principal
  title: text 1..80
  open: bool
  immutable owner
  public 'club titles and whether enrolment is open are public metadata'
  insert <- .owner = viewer
  update <- .owner = viewer
  online only
table catalogueMembers:
  club: clubCatalogue
  principal: principal
  unique club, principal
  immutable club, principal
  read <- .principal = viewer
  insert <- .principal = viewer and exists clubCatalogue[.club, open = true]
  online only
table clubSheets:
  club: clubCatalogue
  owner: principal
  body: text 1..200
  unique club
  immutable club, owner
  read <- .owner = viewer or exists catalogueMembers[.club, viewer]
  insert <- .owner = viewer and exists clubCatalogue[.club, owner = .owner]
  online only
mutation catalogueClub(title: text 1..80, open: bool, body: text 1..200):
  c = insert clubCatalogue { owner: viewer, title, open }
  insert clubSheets { club: c.id, owner: viewer, body }
  return c
mutation selfJoinClub(club: clubCatalogue):
  return insert catalogueMembers { club, principal: viewer }
query catalogueClubById(club: clubCatalogue):
  return clubCatalogue[club]
query clubSheet(club: clubCatalogue):
  return clubSheets[club = club]
```

//! Lowering one Quarry program to the IR: a scope of typed names, one
//! statement list that expressions may hoist temporaries into, reads that
//! choose their index, writes that read their target once, and shapes that
//! become bounded loops. Refusals carry verified repairs or prose guidance.

use super::catalog::{Catalog, TableInfo};
use crate::ast::{self, Expr as A, Literal, ObjectField, ProgramKind, ReadExpr, ShapeField, Stmt as S, WriteOp};
use crate::diag::{Diagnostic, Fallible, Site, ambiguous, name_rewrite, placement, type_error, unbound_loop, unknown, unsupported};
use crate::print;
use crate::types::{Ty, scalar_to_arg};
use snapback4_core::ir::{self, BinaryOp, Bound, Builtin, Expr, IndexKeyExpr, Input, KeyProvenance, LoopDomain, Merge, MergeLane, Order, Program, Read, Recurse, Scan, SortKey, Stmt, UnaryOp};
use snapback4_core::value::Value;
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

mod control;
mod job_reads;
mod pure_calls;
mod point_facts;
pub(super) mod maintain_types;
mod flow;
mod conditional;
mod joins;
mod returned_shapes;
mod shape_meter;
mod diagnostics;
mod constraints;
mod expr;
mod extrema;
mod objects;
mod write_keys;
mod system_principal;
mod denied_writes;
mod keys;
mod reads;
mod prefix;
mod lanes;
mod views;
pub(crate) mod setup;

/// A bound operation: the IR plus the types the client sees.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct BoundProgram {
    /// Source statement ownership stays beside flat IR, like diagnostic sites.
    pub(crate) statement_ends: Vec<usize>,
    pub(crate) maintenance_scopes: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    pub view_types: BTreeMap<String, Ty>,
    pub(crate) filters: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    /// Whole-domain consumers for diagnostics only; absent from IR/wire.
    pub(crate) constraint_domains: std::collections::BTreeSet<String>,
    pub(crate) sources: BTreeMap<String, (Site, String)>,
    pub(crate) receipt_sources: Vec<(Expr, Site)>,
    pub(crate) receipt_returns: Vec<(String, Site)>,
    pub(crate) implicit_write_results: Vec<Expr>,
    /// Source-table pages before binding filters, for executable horizon repairs only.
    pub(crate) page_sources: BTreeMap<String, ReadExpr>,
    pub(crate) canonical_sources: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    pub(crate) selections: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    /// Scan sites the compiler generated for a timeline read: the only scans whose
    /// filter is exactly the platform's retirement check.
    pub(crate) timeline_scans: std::collections::BTreeSet<String>,
    pub program: Program,
    pub(crate) presence: Option<snapback4_core::schema::Execution>,
    pub module: String,
    pub args: Vec<(String, Ty)>,
    pub result: Ty,
    pub notes: Vec<Diagnostic>,
    pub site: Site,
    /// Job helpers with reads that need a non-uniform meter in a query too; never IR/wire.
    pub job_bound_helpers: std::collections::BTreeSet<String>,
}

impl BoundProgram {
    pub fn filter_source(&self, site: &str) -> Option<&str> { self.filters.get(site).map(String::as_str) }
    pub fn domain_source(&self, site: &str) -> Option<&str> { self.sources.get(&format!("domain/{site}")).map(|(_, text)| text.as_str()) }
    pub fn loop_source(&self, binding: &str) -> Option<&str> { self.sources.get(&format!("for/{binding}")).map(|(_, text)| text.as_str()) }
    pub fn selections(&self) -> &BTreeMap<String, String> { &self.selections }
    /// Whether the scan at `site` is a timeline read's own expansion, not an authored scan.
    pub fn timeline_scan(&self, site: &str) -> bool { self.timeline_scans.contains(site) }
    /// The actual binder choice, shared by `why` and structural cost reports.
    pub fn selection(&self, site: &str) -> Option<&str> { self.selections.get(site).map(String::as_str) }
}

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct Binder<'a> {
    viewer_depth: Option<u32>,
    pure_functions: BTreeMap<String, bool>,
    maintenance_scopes: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    call_types: BTreeMap<Vec<String>, Ty>,
    filters: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    view_types: BTreeMap<String, Ty>,
    constraint_domains: std::collections::BTreeSet<String>,
    sources: BTreeMap<String, (Site, String)>,
    page_sources: BTreeMap<String, ReadExpr>,
    canonical_sources: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    selections: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    presence_sources: Vec<(Expr, Site, String)>,
    receipt_sources: Vec<(Expr, Site)>,
    receipt_returns: Vec<(String, Site)>,
    implicit_write_results: Vec<Expr>,
    catalog: &'a Catalog,
    schema: &'a snapback4_core::schema::Schema,
    name: String,
    kind: ProgramKind,
    caller_kind: ProgramKind,
    /// Diagnostic-only caller path; helpers never change execution authority.
    call_chain: Vec<(String, Site)>,
    job_bound_helpers: std::collections::BTreeSet<String>,
    scopes: Vec<BTreeMap<String, Binding>>,
    focal: Option<(String, Ty)>,
    /// Only direct scanned-row columns get false-on-null ordering in where.
    in_predicate: bool,
    returned_data: bool,
    shape_field: Option<(String, Site)>,
    variants: Vec<(String, bool)>,
    temps: u32,
    sites: u32,
    returns: Vec<(Ty, A)>,
    /// Depth of helper expansion, to bound recursion.
    depth: u32,
    /// Enclosing `for` and `chunk` bodies: where `break` may stand.
    loops: u32,
    /// Binding facts carried by the current loop's transfer edges. Ordinary
    /// fallthrough remains in the lexical scope; these edges meet their own
    /// destinations at the loop header or exit.
    loop_transfers: Vec<LoopTransfers>,
    /// Enclosing caller loops affect placement, but never helper-local jumps.
    caller_loops: u32,
    in_prelude: bool,
    /// The total being bound (`count`, or `sum` and its column), for E_QUARRY_BOUND's view recipe.
    total: Option<(&'static str, Option<String>)>,
    record_ownership: bool,
    /// The feed table and source site of the timeline read being expanded, while
    /// it is: the generated scan carries that read's own site.
    expanding_feed: Option<(String, Site)>,
    timeline_scans: std::collections::BTreeSet<String>,
    notes: Vec<Diagnostic>,
    errors: Vec<Diagnostic>,
    /// Totals over partly readable rows that a readable grant may decide:
    /// refused only if the finished program does not guard the scan with it.
    pending_totals: Vec<(String, Diagnostic)>,
}

/// Source names are lexical; the IR's flat environment needs a distinct
/// runtime name for each declaration and each helper expansion.
#[derive(Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
struct Binding {
    runtime: String,
    ty: Ty,
    declared: Ty,
    parameter: bool,
    revision: u32,
    fields: BTreeMap<Vec<String>, Ty>,
    /// An invalid binding retains its original cause; its unknown type never escapes compilation.
    error: Option<Diagnostic>,
    /// Original operands for a diagnostic rewrite, never type or runtime provenance.
    origins: Vec<(Ty, A)>,
}

#[derive(Clone, Default)]
struct LoopTransfers {
    /// Binding state is keyed by runtime identity so a same-named loop item
    /// cannot hide an outer local from the header or exit meet.
    breaks: Vec<BTreeMap<String, Binding>>,
    continues: Vec<BTreeMap<String, Binding>>,
}

const MENTION_MAX: u32 = 64;

pub enum Literal {

}

}

//! The catalog: every table (declared or generated for a maintain) with its
//! columns and indexes, plus the named shapes and functions, and where each
//! schema path was declared so a core validation issue can point at a line.

use crate::ast::*;

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct TableInfo {
    pub through: Option<snapback4_core::schema::through::Through>,
    pub captures: BTreeMap<String, ScalarType>,
    pub recorded: BTreeMap<String, ScalarType>,

    pub columns: BTreeMap<String, ScalarType>,

}

impl TableInfo {
    pub fn total_unique(&self, index: &IndexDecl) -> bool {
        index.unique && index.is_plain() && index.columns.iter().all(|column| column == "id" || self.columns.get(column).is_some_and(|ty| !ty.optional))
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.013#2.1 — a scan cursor carries declared components only;
    // uniqueness with a nullable component cannot resolve ties between nulls.
    pub fn total_cursor(&self, index: &IndexDecl) -> bool {
        index.columns.iter().any(|column| column == "id") || index.unique && index.words.is_none()
            && index.columns.iter().all(|column| match index.computed.get(column) {
                Some(expr) => super::time_indexes::key(&self.decl.name, &self.columns, column, expr).is_ok(),
                None => self.columns.get(column).is_some_and(|ty| !ty.optional),
            })
    }
}

//! Published authorization examples execute under their authored rules.
use snapback4_core::{Context, Engine, Value, WriteState};
use snapback4_lang::guide;
const RECIPES: &str = include_str!("../src/guide/rule_recipes.md");
fn engine(marker: &str) -> Engine {
    let source = guide::quarry(RECIPES)
        .into_iter()
        .find(|s| s.contains(marker))
        .unwrap();
    let b = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source)]);
    assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{marker}: {:?}", b.diagnostics);
    assert!(b.notes.is_empty(), "{marker}: {:?}", b.notes);
    Engine::memory(b.schema, b.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap()
}
fn id(s: &str) -> Value {
    Value::Id(s.into())
}
fn text(s: &str) -> Value {
    Value::String(s.into())
}
fn ctx(w: &str, p: &[(&str, Value)]) -> Context {
    Context::new(
        w,
        p.iter().map(|(k, v)| (k.to_string(), v.clone())).collect(),
    )
    .at(1000)
}
fn row(t: &str, p: &[(&str, Value)]) -> (String, Value) {
    (
        t.into(),
        snapback4_core::value::object(p.iter().map(|(k, v)| (*k, v.clone()))),
    )
}
fn write(e: &mut Engine, op: &str, w: &str, p: &[(&str, Value)]) -> Value {
    match e.mutate(op, None, ctx(w, p)).unwrap().state {
        WriteState::Sent { result, .. } => result,
        WriteState::Failed { why, .. } => panic!("{op}: {} {}", why.code, why.message),
    }
}
fn read(e: &mut Engine, op: &str, w: &str, p: &[(&str, Value)]) -> Value {
    e.query(op, ctx(w, p)).unwrap().data
}
fn refused(e: &mut Engine, op: &str, w: &str, p: &[(&str, Value)]) {
    assert!(matches!(
        e.mutate(op, None, ctx(w, p)).unwrap().state,
        WriteState::Failed { .. }
    ));
}
#[test]
fn clean_recipes() {
    for m in [
        "table folders:",
        "table agreements:",
        "table drafts:",
        "table tokens:",
        "table responses:",
        "table bands:",
        "table library:",
    ] {
        engine(m);
    }
}
#[test]
fn copied_audience() {
    let mut e = engine("table folders:");
    let f = write(&mut e, "addFolder", "alice", &[])
        .field("id")
        .unwrap()
        .clone();
    let r = write(
        &mut e,
        "addFile",
        "alice",
        &[("folder", f.clone()), ("title", text("private"))],
    )
    .field("id")
    .unwrap()
    .clone();
    assert_eq!(
        read(&mut e, "file", "bob", &[("file", r.clone())]),
        Value::Null
    );
    assert_eq!(
        read(&mut e, "file", "alice", &[("file", r)]).field("title"),
        Ok(&text("private"))
    );
    refused(
        &mut e,
        "addFile",
        "bob",
        &[("folder", f), ("title", text("wrong"))],
    );
}
#[test]
fn consent_revocation() {
    let mut e = engine("table agreements:");
    e.seed([row(
        "agreements",
        &[
            ("id", id("a")),
            ("owner", id("owner")),
            ("first", id("alice")),
            ("second", id("bob")),
        ],
    )])
    .unwrap();
    let p = [("agreement", id("a"))];
    write(&mut e, "consent", "alice", &p);
    refused(&mut e, "open", "owner", &p);
    write(&mut e, "consent", "bob", &p);
    write(&mut e, "open", "owner", &p);
    assert_eq!(read(&mut e, "granted", "owner", &p), Value::Bool(true));
    assert_eq!(read(&mut e, "granted", "outsider", &p), Value::Bool(false));
    write(&mut e, "withdraw", "alice", &p);
    assert_eq!(read(&mut e, "granted", "owner", &p), Value::Bool(false));
}
#[test]
fn event_reveal() {
    let mut e = engine("table drafts:");
    let d = write(&mut e, "draft", "alice", &[("body", text("secret"))])
        .field("id")
        .unwrap()
        .clone();
    refused(&mut e, "reveal", "bob", &[("draft", d.clone())]);
    let r = write(&mut e, "reveal", "alice", &[("draft", d)])
        .field("id")
        .unwrap()
        .clone();
    assert_eq!(
        read(&mut e, "publication", "bob", &[("publication", r)]).field("body"),
        Ok(&text("secret"))
    );
}
#[test]
fn token_enrollment() {
    let mut e = engine("table tokens:");
    let secret = "012345678901234567890123456789012345";
    e.seed([
        row(
            "documents",
            &[
                ("id", id("d")),
                ("owner", id("alice")),
                ("body", text("linked")),
            ],
        ),
        row(
            "tokens",
            &[
                ("id", id("t")),
                ("document", id("d")),
                ("secret", text(secret)),
            ],
        ),
    ])
    .unwrap();
    let p = [("document", id("d"))];
    assert_eq!(read(&mut e, "document", "bob", &p), Value::Null);
    refused(
        &mut e,
        "claim",
        "bob",
        &[("secret", text("00000000000000000000000000000000"))],
    );
    write(&mut e, "claim", "bob", &[("secret", text(secret))]);
    assert_eq!(
        read(&mut e, "document", "bob", &p).field("body"),
        Ok(&text("linked"))
    );
    assert_eq!(read(&mut e, "document", "carol", &p), Value::Null);
    write(
        &mut e,
        "revokeLink",
        "service:ops",
        &[("secret", text(secret))],
    );
    refused(&mut e, "claim", "carol", &[("secret", text(secret))]);
    assert_eq!(
        read(&mut e, "document", "bob", &p).field("body"),
        Ok(&text("linked"))
    );
    write(
        &mut e,
        "revokeReader",
        "service:ops",
        &[("document", id("d")), ("principal", id("bob"))],
    );
    assert_eq!(read(&mut e, "document", "bob", &p), Value::Null);
}
#[test]
fn split_anonymity() {
    let mut e = engine("table responses:");
    let r = write(&mut e, "respond", "alice", &[("body", text("anonymous"))]);
    assert!(r.field("principal").is_err());
    let p = [("response", r.field("id").unwrap().clone())];
    assert_eq!(read(&mut e, "author", "bob", &p), Value::Null);
    assert_eq!(
        read(&mut e, "author", "alice", &p).field("principal"),
        Ok(&id("alice"))
    );

//! Expression lowering with branch-owned setup statements.

use super::*;
use snapback4_core::calendar::Civil;

impl Binder<'_> {
    /// Bare tables are domains only at an aggregate/loop consumer; ordinary
    /// names keep their lexical meaning and ordinary pages keep their bounds.
    pub(super) fn aggregate_domain(&self, value: &A) -> Option<ReadExpr> {
        match value {
            A::Read(read) if read.range.is_none() && read.matching.is_none() => Some(read.clone()),
            A::Name { name, site } if self.binding(name).is_none() && self.catalog.tables.contains_key(name) =>
                Some(ReadExpr { cursor_prefix: None, order_keys: Vec::new(), computed: Default::default(), table: name.clone(), keys: Vec::new(), index: Some("byId".into()), range: None, matching: None, filter: None, seeks: Vec::new(), site: site.clone() }),
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    // ----- expressions -----

    /// An expression whose value is used (bound, returned, or a final
    /// write): writes yield their row.
    pub(super) fn value_of(&mut self, value: &A, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
        if let A::Write { .. } = value {
            return self.write(value, out, true).map(|opt| opt.expect("a valued write"));
        }
        self.expr(value, out)
    }

    /// Strict compound children run in source order, before setup emitted by
    /// a later child. Lexical values and their fields cannot change here;
    /// leaving those fields in their object also preserves pending provenance.
    pub(super) fn snapshot(&mut self, value: Expr, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Expr {
        if matches!(value, Expr::Literal(_) | Expr::Input(Input::Viewer | Input::Now)) || lexical_value(&value) {
            return value;
        }
        let name = self.temp("value");
        out.push(Stmt::Let { name: name.clone(), value });
        ir::var(&name)
    }

    pub(super) fn sequence_setup<'e>(&mut self, prior: impl Iterator<Item = &'e mut Expr>, setup: setup::Body, out: &mut setup::Body) {
        if !setup.is_empty() {
            for value in prior {
                *value = self.snapshot(std::mem::replace(value, Expr::Literal(Value::Null)), out);
            }
            out.extend(setup);
        }
    }

    pub(super) fn strict_args(&mut self, args: &[A], out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<Vec<(Expr, Ty)>> {
        let mut values = Vec::<(Expr, Ty)>::new();
        let mut failed = None;
        for arg in args {
            let mut setup = setup::Body::default();
            match self.expr(arg, &mut setup) {
                Ok(value) => {
                    self.sequence_setup(values.iter_mut().map(|(e, _)| e), setup, out);
                    values.push(value);
                }
                Err(error) => { self.record_error(error.clone()); failed.get_or_insert(error); }
            }
        }
        match failed { Some(error) => Err(error), None => Ok(values) }
    }

    pub(super) fn expr(&mut self, expr: &A, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
        // Only shape/object/list construction forwards output position. Every
        // operator, call, key, predicate and assertion consumes its operands.
        let prior = self.returned_data;
        self.returned_data &= matches!(expr, A::Array { .. } | A::Object { .. } | A::Shape { .. } | A::ShapeUse { .. } | A::Member { .. } | A::Name { .. } | A::Focal { .. });
        let start = out.len();
        let result = self.expr_inner(expr, out);
        if !matches!(expr, A::Write { .. }) && point_facts::writes(&out[start..]) { self.invalidate_points(); }
        self.returned_data = prior;
        // Even a builtin's ordinary type refusal should explain why a whole
        // maintained value cannot be consumed. Successful calls are checked by
        // the shared IR allowlist; this only improves already-failing calls.
        if result.as_ref().is_err_and(|d| d.code == "E_QUARRY_TYPE") {
            let args: Vec<&A> = match expr { A::Call { args, .. } => args.iter().collect(), A::Binary { left, right, .. } => vec![left, right], _ => vec![] };
            let mut probe = self.clone();
            for arg in args {
                if let Ok((_, ty)) = probe.expr(arg, &mut setup::Body::default()) {
                    self.maintenance_operand(&ty, arg.site())?;
                }
            }
        }
        let result = result?;
        if !self.schema.receipts.is_empty() {
            let site = self.call_chain.iter().find(|(name, _)| self.catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d| &d.tables).filter_map(|t| t.receipts.as_ref()).any(|clause| &clause.operations[0] == name))
                .map(|(_, site)| site).unwrap_or_else(|| expr.site());
            self.receipt_sources.push((result.0.clone(), site.clone()));
        }
        Ok(result)
    }

    fn maintenance_operand(&self, ty: &Ty, site: &Site) -> Fallible<()> {
        match ty {
            Ty::Row(table) | Ty::WriteRow(table) | Ty::Page(table, _) => {
                if self.schema.row_view_predicate_field(table, "recountPending").is_some() {
                    return Err(Diagnostic::new(site, "E_VIEW_KEY", format!("value contains {table}.recountPending/pending maintenance state"),
                        "project the field you need first (value, member identity or copied member fields); return maintenance state only as query data").guide("maintains"));
                }
            },
            Ty::Optional(item) | Ty::List(item, _) | Ty::Collection(item, _) => self.maintenance_operand(item, site)?,
            Ty::Object(fields) | Ty::RowShape(fields) | Ty::RowObject(fields) => for ty in fields.values() { self.maintenance_operand(ty, site)?; },
            _ => {},
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    pub(super) fn computation(&mut self, expr: &A, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
        let prior = std::mem::replace(&mut self.returned_data, false);
        let result = self.expr(expr, out);
        self.returned_data = prior;
        result
    }

    fn expr_inner(&mut self, expr: &A, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
        let site = expr.site();
        match expr {
            A::Name { name, .. } if self.binding(name).is_none() && self.catalog.view_reads.contains_key(name) => {
                let mut merge = self.catalog.view_reads[name].clone();
                merge.site = site.clone();
                self.planned_view(&merge, out, prefix::Position::Page)
            }
            A::Name { name, .. } if self.binding(name).is_none() && self.catalog.tables.contains_key(name) => {
                self.read(&ReadExpr { cursor_prefix: None, computed: Default::default(), table: name.clone(), keys: Vec::new(), seeks: Vec::new(), filter: None, index: Some("byId".into()),
                    order_keys: vec!["id".into()], range: None, matching: None, site: site.clone() }, out)
            }
            A::Name { name, .. } => self.name(name, site),
            A::Focal { .. } => match &self.focal {
                Some((var, ty)) => Ok((ir::var(var), ty.clone())),
                None => Err(unknown(site, "focal . is valid only inside a row shape", "name the row: `row.field`")),
            },
            A::Literal { value, .. } => Ok(match value {
                Literal::Str(text) => (Expr::Literal(Value::String(text.clone())), Ty::Text(text.chars().count() as u32)),
                Literal::Number(text) => (Expr::Literal(Value::Int(super::super::integer_literal(text, site)?)), Ty::Int),
                Literal::Bool(b) => (Expr::Literal(Value::Bool(*b)), Ty::Bool),
                Literal::Null => (Expr::Literal(Value::Null), Ty::Null),
            }),
            A::Array { items, .. } => {
                let mut exprs = Vec::new();
                let mut item_ty: Option<Ty> = None;
                let args = self.strict_args(items, out)?;
                let origins: Vec<_> = args.iter().zip(items).map(|((_, ty), source)| (ty.clone(), source.clone())).collect();
                for (e, t) in args {
                    item_ty = Some(match item_ty {
                        Some(j) => j.join_item(&t).map_err(|error| self.join_error_many(error.at("[]"), &origins))?,
                        None => t,
                    });
                    exprs.push(e);
                }
                let max = exprs.len() as u32;
                Ok((Expr::Array(exprs), Ty::List(Box::new(item_ty.unwrap_or(Ty::Unknown)), max)))
            }

query itemsOf(list: lists, c: cursor ?):

//! `t where .col = value shapeName`: the postfix shape parses onto the last
//! operand, but the builder shapes the page. Hoisting it before planning keeps
//! the equality a prefix pin, so a declared shape never changes which index or
//! cap covers the read (app-farm wave 5, lane 100).
// @ref LLP 3000.036#15.1 — a shape suffix must not change prefix coverage.
use super::{catalog::{Catalog, TableInfo}, intent::independent, intent_walk as walk};
use crate::ast::*;
use std::collections::BTreeSet;

pub(super) fn hoist(catalog: &mut Catalog) {
    let shapes: BTreeSet<String> = catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|doc| doc.shapes.iter().map(|shape| shape.name.clone())).collect();
    if shapes.is_empty() { return; }
    let tables = &catalog.tables;
    for doc in &mut catalog.documents {
        walk::document(doc, &mut |value| {
            let Expr::Read(read) = value else { return };
            let Some(table) = tables.get(&read.table) else { return };
            let Some(filter) = read.filter.as_mut() else { return };
            let Some((name, site)) = take_trailing(filter, &shapes, table) else { return };
            let read = std::mem::replace(read, empty(&site));
            *value = Expr::ShapeUse { base: Box::new(Expr::Read(read)), name, site };
        });
    }
}

fn empty(site: &crate::diag::Site) -> ReadExpr {
    ReadExpr { table: String::new(), keys: Vec::new(), index: None, site: site.clone(), cursor_prefix: None, computed: Default::default(),
        order_keys: Vec::new(), range: None, matching: None, filter: None, seeks: Vec::new() }
}

/// Removes the rightmost postfix shape when it ends a comparison of a scalar
/// column with a row-independent operand (an argument, a literal). A shaped
/// value is an object, and an object never compares with a scalar column, so
/// the shape can only mean the page. Anything else (an object compared with an
/// object, a row-dependent operand) keeps the binder's typed recognition.
fn take_trailing(expr: &mut Expr, shapes: &BTreeSet<String>, table: &TableInfo) -> Option<(String, crate::diag::Site)> {
    let Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = expr else { return None };
    if op == "and" { return take_trailing(right, shapes, table); }
    if !matches!(op.as_str(), "=" | "!=" | "<" | "<=" | ">" | ">=") || !scalar_column(left, table) { return None; }
    let Expr::ShapeUse { base, name, site } = right.as_mut() else { return None };
    if !shapes.contains(name) || !independent(base) { return None; }
    let found = (name.clone(), site.clone());
    **right = *base.clone();
    Some(found)
}

fn scalar_column(value: &Expr, table: &TableInfo) -> bool {
    let Expr::Member { base, name, .. } = value else { return false };
    if !matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Focal { .. }) { return false; }
    name == "id" || table.columns.get(name).is_some_and(|column| matches!(column.kind,
        TypeKind::Text { .. } | TypeKind::Id | TypeKind::Principal | TypeKind::Int | TypeKind::IntRange { .. } | TypeKind::Decimal { .. } | TypeKind::Money { .. }
        | TypeKind::Time | TypeKind::Bool | TypeKind::Enum { .. } | TypeKind::Ref { .. }))
}
//! File-qualified helper calls lower to the same global declaration before
//! planner/provenance analysis. Qualification never creates a second helper.
use crate::{ast::*, diag::Diagnostic, print};
use std::{collections::BTreeMap, path::Path};

pub(super) fn normalize(documents: &mut [Document], diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {
    let owners: BTreeMap<_, _> = documents.iter().flat_map(|doc| {
        let module = Path::new(&doc.file).file_stem().and_then(|s| s.to_str()).unwrap_or("schema");
        doc.functions.iter().map(move |f| (f.name.clone(), module.to_owned()))
    }).collect();
    let mut visit = |value: &mut Expr| {
        let Expr::Call { name, args, site } = value else { return; };
        // @ref LLP 3000.007#23 — aliases lower before planning; an explicitly declared helper keeps its meaning.
        if matches!(name.as_str(), "len" | "length") && !owners.contains_key(name) { *name = "count".into(); }
        let Some((module, helper)) = name.split_once('.') else { return; };
        let Some(owner) = owners.get(helper) else { return; };
        if module != owner {
            diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(site, "E_QUARRY_UNKNOWN",
                format!("helper {helper} is declared in {owner}.q, not {module}.q"),
                format!("{owner}.{helper}({})", args.iter().map(print::expression).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "))).guide("programs"));
        }
        *name = helper.to_owned();
    };
    for doc in documents {
        super::intent_walk::document(doc, &mut visit);
        for view in &mut doc.views {
            for branch in &mut view.branches {
                if let Some(p) = &mut branch.predicate { super::intent_walk::expression(p, &mut visit); }
                super::intent_walk::shape(&mut branch.fields, &mut visit);
            }
        }
    }
}

            S::For { name, domain, body, site } => {
                if self.caller_kind == ProgramKind::Job && self.loops + self.caller_loops == 0
                    && matches!(domain, A::If { condition, .. } if super::super::intent_variants::condition(condition).is_some()) {
                    let mut leaf = domain;
                    while let A::If { then, .. } = leaf { leaf = then; }
                    let table = match leaf { A::Read(read) => read.table.as_str(), A::Merge(merge) => merge.table.as_str(), _ => "table" };
                    return Err(placement(site, "argument-presence alternatives cannot be a job sweep domain",
                        format!("for {name} in {table}:  -- put the optional filter and work inside this stable sweep")).guide("jobs"));
                }
                let (loop_domain, item_ty) = self.loop_domain(domain, out)?;
                if self.binding(name).is_some_and(|binding| binding.parameter) {
                    return Err(unknown(site, format!("{name} is an argument; locals cannot shadow arguments"), "give the loop item a fresh name"));
                }
                let before = self.visible_bindings();
                let (binding, body_out) = self.loop_body(name, item_ty, body, site, matches!(loop_domain, LoopDomain::Page(_)) && !self.source_may_write(body))?;
                if self.caller_kind == ProgramKind::Job && self.loops + self.caller_loops == 0 && matches!(&loop_domain, LoopDomain::Page(scan) if scan.take == Bound::Meter) {
                    let after = self.visible_bindings();
                    if let Some((name, _)) = before.iter().find(|(name, old)| !name.starts_with('$') && after.get(*name).is_some_and(|new| old.revision != new.revision)) {
                        return Err(placement(site, format!("automatic sweep carries local {name} across iterations, but chunks commit independently"), format!("declare {name} inside the `for row in table:` body for per-item work, or store cross-chunk progress in a table")).guide("jobs"));
                    }
                }
                self.sources.insert(format!("for/{binding}"), (site.clone(), self.catalog.source.expression(domain)));
                out.push_for(binding, loop_domain, body_out);
                Ok(())
            }
            S::Break { site } => {
                if self.loops == 0 {
                    if self.viewer_depth.is_some() { return Err(job_reads::diagnostic(site, "E_JOB_VIEWER_FLOW", "break cannot leave an as block or helper", "put break in a loop inside this block or helper")); }
                    return Err(placement(site, "break ends the enclosing for or chunk, and there is none here", "delete this line; a program ends with return"));
                }
                let bindings = self.binding_states();
                self.loop_transfers.last_mut().expect("an enclosing loop").breaks.push(bindings);
                out.push(Stmt::Break);

- **[P2] Named shapes can preserve stale snapshot evidence.** Effect detection at [rule_composition.rs:311](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-w12-rules2/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/rule_composition.rs:311) counts only explicit writes/calls. The walker traverses a shape use’s base, without its definition ([intent_walk.rs:7](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-w12-rules2/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/intent_walk.rs:7)), while runtime binding executes its fields. For a publicly readable row, assert a snapshot, require `r.owner != viewer`, then apply a named shape whose helper transfers ownership to `viewer`. A subsequent update under `next.owner = viewer` succeeds but receives a false `N_WRITE_DENIED` from the stale snapshot. Named spreads have the same gap. Conservatively invalidate evidence for opaque shape expansions, or inspect their transitive effects, including in if conditions.
